EXECUTE and VERIFY left artefacts that\r\nwere checked; PLAN left nothing, so nothing noticed that it had never been dispatched — across every round\r\nto date, `--lane PLAN` had been invoked zero times while the research was written by the session model,\r\nwhich is the EXECUTE lane's model.\r\n\r\nThis gate makes a lane claim falsifiable. Given a dispatch receipt it verifies, fail-closed:\r\n\r\n  lane            the receipt is for the lane the round claims\r\n  served == asked the attempt reports the model that was actually asked for (no silent substitution)\r\n  rank 1          the first attempt is the in-force primary arm, or --allow-rank names another\r\n  ok + usage      the dispatch succeeded and the usage receipt parsed\r\n  nonce           the round's nonce appears in the receipt\r\n  exit 0          the attempt exited cleanly\r\n\r\nExit 0 = the lane was served as claimed. Exit 2 = it was not, with the reasons.\r\nRead-only.\r\n\"\"\"\r\n\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport argparse\r\nimport json\r\nimport pathlib\r\n\r\n\r\ndef judge(receipt: dict, lane: str, nonce: str | None = None, allow_rank: int = 1) -> dict:\r\n    problems: list[str] = []\r\n    attempts = receipt.get(\"attempts\") or []\r\n    if not attempts:\r\n        return {\"verdict\": \"NO_ATTEMPT\", \"lane\": lane, \"problems\": [\"receipt carries no attempt\"]}\r\n    # Which attempt counts? For a finished receipt the attempt that served is the one the dispatcher\r\n    # accepted; for a provisional receipt there is no such mark, so the LAST attempt is the one the run\r\n    # ended on. Judging attempts[0] told a comfortable story: sitting 6's first attempt served\r\n    # gpt-6.1-sol and failed nothing, while the run actually fell through to rank 4 (nvidia deepseek,\r\n    # exit 1). A gate that reports the first attempt as the served arm is the same defect as a summary\r\n    # that only quotes the good part.\r\n    a = attempts[-1]\r\n\r\n    if receipt.get(\"lane\") != lane:\r\n        problems.append(f\"lane_mismatch: receipt={receipt.get('lane')} claimed={lane}\")\r\n    if a.get(\"rank\") != allow_rank:\r\n        problems.append(f\"rank_not_primary: rank={a.get('rank')} allowed={allow_rank}\")\r\n    if a.get(\"exit\") != 0:\r\n        problems.append(f\"dispatch_exit_nonzero: exit={a.get('exit')}\")\r\n    # A provisional receipt is what survives when the dispatcher is torn down before its final write.\r\n    # It can prove WHICH ARM SERVED (lane, rank, provider/model, exit) -- which is the question this gate\r\n    # exists to answer -- and it cannot prove the turn finished cleanly. The distinction is carried in the\r\n    # verdict rather than flattened, and --require-final refuses it outright.\r\n    provisional = receipt.get(\"receipt_complete\") is False\r\n    fallthrough = len(attempts) > 1\r\n    if provisional:\r\n        a_prov = True\r\n        if a.get(\"exit\") != 0:\r\n            problems.append(f\"provisional_attempt_nonzero: exit={a.get('exit')}\")\r\n        if not a.get(\"provider\") or not a.get(\"model\"):\r\n            problems.append(\"provisional_attempt_without_identity\")\r\n    else:\r\n        a_prov = False\r\n        if not receipt.get(\"ok\"):\r\n            problems.append(\"dispatch_not_ok\")\r\n        if not receipt.get(\"usage_receipt_ok\"):\r\n            problems.append(\"usage_receipt_not_ok\")\r\n\r\n    asked = receipt.get(\"used_transport_model\")\r\n    served = a.get(\"model\")\r\n\r\n    def _arm_pair(value, provider_hint=None):\r\n        \"\"\"(provider, model) from either a qualified id or a bare one plus its provider.\r\n\r\n        MEASURED 2026-10-31 18:54: asked `opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash` vs served model `mimo-v2.6-flash`\r\n        (provider `opencode-go`) was reported as `silent_substitution` -- the receipt's own provider field\r\n        said it was the same arm. That accusation is the most damaging one this gate can make, so it must not\r\n        be produced by a string shape: both sides are normalised to (provider, model) before comparing.\r\n        \"\"\"\r\n        if not value:\r\n            return (None, None)\r\n        value = str(value)\r\n        if \"/\" in value:\r\n            head, _, tail = value.partition(\"/\")\r\n            return (head.strip().lower(), tail.strip().lower())\r\n        return ((str(provider_hint).strip().lower() if provider_hint else None), value.strip().lower())\r\n\r\n    if asked:\r\n        asked_pair = _arm_pair(asked)\r\n        served_pair = _arm_pair(served, a.get(\"provider\"))\r\n        # A bare asked id with no provider of its own compares on the model only; a qualified one must match on\r\n        # both halves, because \"same model name, different provider\" is exactly the substitution under test.\r\n        same = (asked_pair[1] == served_pair[1]\r\n                and (asked_pair[0] is None or asked_pair[0] == served_pair[0]))\r\n        if served and not same:\r\n            problems.append(f\"silent_substitution: asked={asked} served={served} \"\r\n                            f\"(provider={a.get('provider')})\")\r\n\r\n    if nonce:\r\n        blob = json.dumps(receipt, ensure_ascii=False)\r\n        found = nonce in blob\r\n        if not found and receipt.get(\"attempt_log_dir\"):\r\n            logdir = pathlib.Path(receipt[\"attempt_log_dir\"])\r\n            if logdir.is_dir():\r\n                for f in logdir.glob(\"*.txt\"):\r\n                    try:\r\n                        if nonce in f.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\"):\r\n                            found = True\r\n                            break\r\n                    except OSError:\r\n                        continue\r\n        if not found:\r\n            problems.append(f\"nonce_not_echoed: {nonce}\")\r\n\r\n    if provisional and fallthrough:\r\n        problems.append(f\"provisional_fallthrough: {len(attempts)} attempts; the run did not accept the first\")\r\n    if problems:\r\n        verdict = \"LANE_CLAIM_UNSUPPORTED\"\r\n    elif provisional:\r\n        verdict = \"LANE_SERVED_PROVISIONAL\"\r\n    else:\r\n        verdict = \"LANE_SERVED_AS_CLAIMED\"\r\n    return {\r\n        \"verdict\": verdict,\r\n        \"receipt_complete\": not provisional,\r\n        \"lane\": lane,\r\n        \"asked\": asked,\r\n        \"served\": served,\r\n        \"rank\": a.get(\"rank\"),\r\n        \"seconds\": a.get(\"seconds\"),\r\n        \"problems\": problems,\r\n    }\r\n\r\n\r\ndef main() -> int:\r\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"verify that a lane claim is backed by its dispatch receipt\")\r\n    ap.add_argument(\"receipt\", help=\"JSON receipt written by hgk-lane-dispatch --json-out\")\r\n    ap.add_argument(\"--lane\", required=True, help=\"PLAN | EXECUTE | VERIFY_SECURITY\")\r\n    ap.add_argument(\"--nonce\", default=None, help=\"the round nonce that must appear in the receipt\")\r\n    ap.add_argument(\"--allow-rank\", type=int, default=1, help=\"accept this rank as the serving arm\")\r\n    ap.add_argument(\"--json\", action=\"store_true\")\r\n    args = ap.parse_args()\r\n\r\n    path = pathlib.Path(args.receipt)\r\n    if not path.exists():\r\n        print(json.dumps({\"verdict\": \"RECEIPT_MISSING\", \"receipt\": str(path)}))\r\n        return 3\r\n    try:\r\n        receipt = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\r\n    except Exception as exc:\r\n        print(json.dumps({\"verdict\": \"RECEIPT_UNREADABLE\", \"detail\": str(exc)[:200]}))\r\n        return 3\r\n\r\n    out = judge(receipt, args.lane, args.nonce, args.allow_rank)\r\n    out[\"receipt\"] = str(path)\r\n    if args.json:\r\n        print(json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False))\r\n    else:\r\n        print(f\"{out['verdict']}: {args.lane} asked={out.get('asked')} served={out.get('served')} rank={out.get('rank')}\")\r\n        for p in out[\"problems\"]:\r\n            print(f\"  - {p}\")\r\n    return 0 if out[\"verdict\"] == \"LANE_SERVED_AS_CLAIMED\" else 2\r\n\r\n\r\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\r\n    raise SystemExit(main())\r\n\r\nPATH High-risk or release decisions escalate to Human Product Authority.\r\n\r\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\r\n\"\"\"lane_evidence_gate — a round cannot claim a lane it did not dispatch.\r\n\r\nThe mandate \"use three lanes\" lived in the prompt and in no gate. EXECUTE and VERIFY left artefacts that\r\nwere checked; PLAN left nothing, so nothing noticed that it had never been dispatched — across every round\r\nto date, `--lane PLAN` had been invoked zero times while the research was written by the session model,\r\nwhich is the EXECUTE lane's model.\r\n\r\nThis gate makes a lane claim falsifiable. Given a dispatch receipt it verifies, fail-closed:\r\n\r\n  lane            the receipt is for the lane the round claims\r\n  served == asked the attempt reports the model that was actually asked for (no silent substitution)\r\n  rank 1          the first attempt is the in-force primary arm, or --allow-rank names another\r\n  ok + usage      the dispatch succeeded and the usage receipt parsed\r\n  nonce           the round's nonce appears in the receipt\r\n  exit 0          the attempt exited cleanly\r\n\r\nExit 0 = the lane was served as claimed. Exit 2 = it was not, with the reasons.\r\nRead-only.\r\n\"\"\"\r\n\r\nfrom __future__ import annotations\r\n\r\nimport argparse\r\nimport json\r\nimport pathlib\r\n\r\n\r\ndef judge(receipt: dict, lane: str, nonce: str | None = None, allow_rank: int = 1) -> dict:\r\n    problems: list[str] = []\r\n    attempts = receipt.get(\"attempts\") or []\r\n    if not attempts:\r\n        return {\"verdict\": \"NO_ATTEMPT\", \"lane\": lane, \"problems\": [\"receipt carries no attempt\"]}\r\n    # Which attempt counts? For a finished receipt the attempt that served is the one the dispatcher\r\n    # accepted; for a provisional receipt there is no such mark, so the LAST attempt is the one the run\r\n    # ended on. Judging attempts[0] told a comfortable story: sitting 6's first attempt served\r\n    # gpt-6.1-sol and failed nothing, while the run actually fell through to rank 4 (nvidia deepseek,\r\n    # exit 1). A gate that reports the first attempt as the served arm is the same defect as a summary\r\n    # that only quotes the good part.\r\n    a = attempts[-1]\r\n\r\n    if receipt.get(\"lane\") != lane:\r\n        problems.append(f\"lane_mismatch: receipt={receipt.get('lane')} claimed={lane}\")\r\n    if a.get(\"rank\") != allow_rank:\r\n        problems.append(f\"rank_not_primary: rank={a.get('rank')} allowed={allow_rank}\")\r\n    if a.get(\"exit\") != 0:\r\n        problems.append(f\"dispatch_exit_nonzero: exit={a.get('exit')}\")\r\n    # A provisional receipt is what survives when the dispatcher is torn down before its final write.\r\n    # It can prove WHICH ARM SERVED (lane, rank, provider/model, exit) -- which is the question this gate\r\n    # exists to answer -- and it cannot prove the turn finished cleanly.
